rating: 5
review_text: If you are in Gulf Shores, AL, you have GOT to stop here at COASTAL.
Here, you will have some awesome food with the most amazing view!!
The waitress we had was sweet, but she was a little slow to check on us throughout our meal, so that's why I gave service a 4 star instead of 5. But, it was nowhere near the worst service we've ever had.
I got the Stuffed Blueberry French Toast (and I paid a little extra for a side of bacon and breakfast potatoes) and my husband got their Signature Coastal Burger (which was super flavorful with the bacon, sautéed onions, bacon aioli, and wild mushroom demi glace *heart eyes*). The view was UNBEATABLE and there's beach access from the restaurant. If you plan ahead a little, you could have an awesome meal with an unbeatable view and then meander on down to the beach for a nice walk or swim!
There's a giftshop on site, as well! It's a bit expensive, but they had some deals going on for some of their items, which made it a little more affordable.

This is one of those places we would want to definitely go back to next time we're in town and we will be suggesting it to our friends and family for their visits!

--- entity_reviews [row 1 of 5] ---
id: 3444eaad-3e50-475f-a882-ac278f18788f
entity_slug: coastal-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Dustin New
rating: 5
body: We had a great experience at Coastal Orange Beach. So nice, we went twice. I was there with my dad and my two sisters, and everything about the visit was excellent. The food was fantastic, with great flavor and quality across the board.
What really stood out was the service. The staff was friendly, attentive, and made us feel welcome from the moment we arrived. They kept everything running smoothly and helped make our family meal even more enjoyable.
If you're in the Gulf Shores or Orange Beach area and looking for a great place to eat, I highly recommend Coastal Orange Beach. We'll definitely be back.
verified_purchase: false
helpful_count: 0
approved: true
created_at: 2026-05-29T01:41:26.196734+00:00

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: e36b05c0-f81a-4a91-bb9d-a4c957aa2231
entity_slug: coastal-orange-beach
reviewer_name: CJ's Adventures
rating: 5
body: If you are in Gulf Shores, AL, you have GOT to stop here at COASTAL.
Here, you will have some awesome food with the most amazing view!!
The waitress we had was sweet, but she was a little slow to check on us throughout our meal, so that's why I gave service a 4 star instead of 5. But, it was nowhere near the worst service we've ever had.
I got the Stuffed Blueberry French Toast (and I paid a little extra for a side of bacon and breakfast potatoes) and my husband got their Signature Coastal Burger (which was super flavorful with the bacon, sautéed onions, bacon aioli, and wild mushroom demi glace *heart eyes*). The view was UNBEATABLE and there's beach access from the restaurant. If you plan ahead a little, you could have an awesome meal with an unbeatable view and then meander on down to the beach for a nice walk or swim!
There's a giftshop on site, as well! It's a bit expensive, but they had some deals going on for some of their items, which made it a little more affordable.

This is one of those places we would want to definitely go back to next time we're in town and we will be suggesting it to our friends and family for their visits!
